### Task 3.6: dsh upgrade canary and canary drill (AC#4)

dsh is an RC-only developer preview with no changelog. The canary is how a breaking
upgrade is discovered by us rather than by production. It never upgrades anything.

**Files:** `jobs/dsh-canary.yaml`, `scripts/canary/run.sh`, `scripts/canary/diff-intel.md`,
`packages/core/src/job.ts` (edit: optional `script` member),
`plugins/helium/src/runtime.ts` (edit: `runScript`), root `package.json` (add `semver`).

- [ ] **Step 1** — Add the optional `script` member to `JobSpec` and its zod schema
      in `packages/core/src/job.ts` (adding members is allowed; renaming is not):

```ts
export interface JobScriptAction {
  command: string; // absolute, or relative to the release root
  args: string[];
  timeoutMs: number;
}
// JobSpec gains:  script?: JobScriptAction;
```

zod: `script: z.object({ command: z.string().min(1), args: z.array(z.string()).default([]),
timeout: z.string() }).optional()` with `timeout` normalised to `timeoutMs` via
`parseDuration`, exactly like the other duration fields. A job carrying `script`
must still declare `engine`, `budget` and `delivery`; the triage/senior engines are
simply unused.

- [ ] **Step 2** — Add `runScript` to `HeliumRuntime` (`runtime.ts`) and route to it
      from `dispatch()` (the `if (job.script …)` line written in Task 3.1 Step 7):

```ts
  private async runScript(job: JobSpec, ev: TriggerEvent): Promise<DispatchResult> {
    const runId = this.ledger.start(job.name, "triage");
    const { command, args, timeoutMs } = job.script!;
    const started = this.nowMs();
    const child = spawn(command, args, {
      cwd: this.deps.config.jobsDir,
      env: { ...process.env, HELIUM_STATE_ROOT: this.deps.config.stateRoot,
             HELIUM_TRIGGER: JSON.stringify(ev) },
      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
    });
    let out = ""; let err = "";
    child.stdout.on("data", (c: Buffer) => { out += c.toString(); });
    child.stderr.on("data", (c: Buffer) => { err += c.toString(); });
    const timer = setTimeout(() => child.kill("SIGTERM"), timeoutMs);
    const code = await new Promise<number>((res) => child.on("close", (c) => res(c ?? -1)));
    clearTimeout(timer);
    const outcome = code === 0 ? "run_completed" : code === null ? "timed_out" : "run_failed";
    this.ledger.finish(runId, job.name, "triage", outcome,
      { code, ms: this.nowMs() - started });
    return { runId, tier: "triage", outcome,
      analysis: out.slice(-8000), error: code === 0 ? undefined : err.slice(-2000) };
  }
```

Import `spawn` from `node:child_process` at the top of `runtime.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 3** — Add `semver` to the root `package.json` dependencies
      (`pnpm add semver && pnpm add -D @types/semver`) — the canary needs a real
      precedence comparison, not string ordering, because RC identifiers sort by rules
      string comparison gets wrong.

- [ ] **Step 4** — Write `jobs/dsh-canary.yaml`. The sentinel is an ordinary sensor:
      `fields: [versions]` makes the poller hash the **entire** `versions` map, so any new
      version key changes the hash. Dist-tag equality is deliberately not used — an
      RC-only package may never move `latest`:

```yaml
name: dsh-canary
enabled: true
triggers:
  - kind: state-change
    url: https://registry.npmjs.org/@deepseek-ai/dsh
    fields: [versions]
    interval: 6h
    dedup_ttl: 24h
  - kind: state-change
    url: https://pypi.org/pypi/deepseek-harness-sdk/json
    fields: [releases]
    interval: 12h
    dedup_ttl: 24h
  - kind: cron
    schedule: "0 9 * * 1"
    tz: America/New_York
engine:
  triage: { engine: deepseek, model: deepseek-v4-flash }
  senior: { engine: claude-max }
escalate_when: critical
session: fresh
memory: none
tools: []
allowMutations: false
max_turns: { triage: 1, senior: 1 }
timeout: 45m
budget: { max_triage_per_hour: 4, max_senior_per_day: 1 }
script:
  command: /Users/moremeds/projects/helium-releases/current/scripts/canary/run.sh
  args: []
  timeout: 40m
delivery:
  jsonl: true
  email:
    to: operator
    subject_prefix: "[helium/canary]"
    max_per_hour: 2
prompt: |
  This job runs scripts/canary/run.sh as a gated script action; no model is called
  by the harness itself. The script's own report is the delivery payload.
```

- [ ] **Step 5** — Write `scripts/canary/run.sh` part 1 (pin, candidate, semver gate):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# dsh upgrade canary. Never upgrades anything. Isolated install + contract suite +
# mirror-repo diff intel -> report -> inbox drop + immediate email.
set -euo pipefail

release="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/../.." && pwd -P)"
STATE_ROOT="${HELIUM_STATE_ROOT:-/Users/moremeds/.helium/state}"
ENV_FILE="${HELIUM_ENV_FILE:-/Users/moremeds/.config/helium/helium.env}"
CACHE="${HELIUM_CANARY_CACHE:-$HOME/.helium/canary}"
REGISTRY="${HELIUM_CANARY_REGISTRY:-https://registry.npmjs.org}"
stamp="$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)"
report="$STATE_ROOT/reports/dsh-canary/$stamp.md"
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$report")" "$STATE_ROOT/inbox" "$CACHE"

pin="$(node -p "require('$release/package.json').dependencies['@deepseek-ai/dsh']")"
candidate="$(node -e '
  const semver=require("semver");
  const list=JSON.parse(require("node:child_process")
    .execSync(`npm view @deepseek-ai/dsh versions --json --registry=${process.argv[2]}`,
      {encoding:"utf8"}));
  const newer=list.filter(v=>semver.gt(v,process.argv[1])).sort(semver.rcompare);
  process.stdout.write(newer[0] ?? "");' "$pin" "$REGISTRY")"

if [ -z "$candidate" ]; then
  echo "canary: no version newer than $pin"
  exit 0
fi
echo "canary: pin=$pin candidate=$candidate"
```

- [ ] **Step 6** — `run.sh` part 2 (throwaway install + contract suite; production is
      never touched because `DSH_HOME` and the workspace are both temporary):

```bash
work="$(mktemp -d -t helium-canary)"
trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
export DSH_HOME="$work/dsh-home"
mkdir -p "$DSH_HOME"

cp -R "$release/packages" "$release/plugins" "$release/contracts" \
      "$release/package.json" "$release/pnpm-workspace.yaml" \
      "$release/pnpm-lock.yaml" "$release/tsconfig.base.json" "$work/"
find "$work" -maxdepth 4 -type d -name node_modules -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true

node -e '
  const {readFileSync,writeFileSync}=require("node:fs");
  const p=JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.argv[1],"utf8"));
  p.pnpm=p.pnpm||{}; p.pnpm.overrides=p.pnpm.overrides||{};
  p.pnpm.overrides["@deepseek-ai/dsh"]=process.argv[2];
  writeFileSync(process.argv[1],JSON.stringify(p,null,2)+"\n");' \
  "$work/package.json" "$candidate"

contracts_status=FAIL
contracts_log="$work/contracts.log"
if ( cd "$work" && pnpm install --no-frozen-lockfile >"$contracts_log" 2>&1 \
     && pnpm build >>"$contracts_log" 2>&1 ); then
  set -a; . "$ENV_FILE"; set +a
  if ( cd "$work" && HELIUM_LIVE=1 HELIUM_DSH_VERSION="$candidate" \
       pnpm -F @helium/contracts test >>"$contracts_log" 2>&1 ); then
    contracts_status=PASS
  fi
fi
echo "canary: contracts=$contracts_status"
```

- [ ] **Step 7** — `run.sh` part 3 (mirror-repo diff intel — the repo URL is read
      from the packument, never hard-coded):

```bash
mirror="$(npm view @deepseek-ai/dsh repository.url --registry="$REGISTRY" 2>/dev/null \
  | sed -e 's#^git+##' -e 's#\.git$##' || true)"
diff_file="$work/diff.patch"
: > "$diff_file"
if [ -n "$mirror" ]; then
  clone="$CACHE/dsh-mirror"
  if [ -d "$clone/.git" ]; then git -C "$clone" fetch --tags --quiet || true
  else git clone --filter=blob:none --quiet "$mirror" "$clone" || true; fi
  resolve_tag() {
    for cand in "v$1" "$1"; do
      git -C "$clone" rev-parse -q --verify "refs/tags/$cand" >/dev/null && { echo "$cand"; return; }
    done
    echo ""
  }
  a="$(resolve_tag "$pin")"; b="$(resolve_tag "$candidate")"
  if [ -n "$a" ] && [ -n "$b" ]; then
    git -C "$clone" diff "$a..$b" -- packages bundles README.md > "$diff_file" || true
  else
    echo "(tags not resolvable in mirror: pin=$a candidate=$b)" > "$diff_file"
  fi
else
  echo "(no repository.url in the packument — diff intel skipped)" > "$diff_file"
fi
head -c 51200 "$diff_file" > "$work/diff.trunc"
```

- [ ] **Step 8** — `run.sh` part 4 (DeepSeek summary via curl, key never in argv —
      it goes into a 0600 `curl -K` config file):

```bash
set -a; . "$ENV_FILE"; set +a
umask 077
curlcfg="$work/curl.cfg"
printf 'header = "Authorization: Bearer %s"\n' "$DEEPSEEK_API_KEY" > "$curlcfg"
model="${HELIUM_CANARY_MODEL:-deepseek-v4-flash}"
node -e '
  const {readFileSync,writeFileSync}=require("node:fs");
  const diff=readFileSync(process.argv[1],"utf8");
  writeFileSync(process.argv[2],JSON.stringify({model:process.argv[3],messages:[
    {role:"system",content:"You review upstream diffs for a downstream integrator."},
    {role:"user",content:
      "helium depends on these dsh seams: cordis plugin apply(), ctx.effect timers, "+
      "ctx.agents.create/followup/whenIdle, the session-event watermark "+
      "(session.seq + assistant/message), ctx.sessions.flush, profile bundles + "+
      "`dsh plugin add file:`, and the dsh web server. Read the diff and answer: "+
      "(1) which of those seams changed, (2) what breaks if we upgrade blind, "+
      "(3) upgrade recommendation in one line. Diff follows.\n\n"+diff}],
    max_tokens:1200}));' "$work/diff.trunc" "$work/req.json" "$model"

intel="$(curl -sS -m 120 -K "$curlcfg" -H 'content-type: application/json' \
  --data @"$work/req.json" https://api.deepseek.com/chat/completions \
  | node -e 'let s="";process.stdin.on("data",d=>s+=d).on("end",()=>{
      try{console.log(JSON.parse(s).choices[0].message.content);}
      catch(e){console.log("(intel call failed: "+String(e)+")");}});')"
rm -f "$curlcfg"
```

- [ ] **Step 9** — `run.sh` part 5 (report, inbox drop, immediate email):

````bash
{
  echo "# dsh canary — $candidate (pinned $pin)"
  echo
  echo "- checked: $stamp"
  echo "- contract suite: **$contracts_status**"
  echo "- mirror: ${mirror:-none}"
  echo "- production profile: untouched (isolated DSH_HOME=$DSH_HOME)"
  echo
  echo "## Change intel"
  echo
  echo "$intel"
  echo
  echo "## Contract log (tail)"
  echo '```'
  tail -40 "$contracts_log" 2>/dev/null || echo "(no log)"
  echo '```'
  echo
  echo "**Never auto-upgrade.** A human promotes the pin in package.json, then ships"
  echo "it as an ordinary release; the canary's isolated profile is the smoke test."
} > "$report"

node -e '
  const {writeFileSync}=require("node:fs");
  writeFileSync(process.argv[1],JSON.stringify({kind:"canary",ts:new Date().toISOString(),
    pin:process.argv[2],candidate:process.argv[3],contracts:process.argv[4],
    report:process.argv[5]},null,2));' \
  "$STATE_ROOT/inbox/canary-$stamp.json" "$pin" "$candidate" "$contracts_status" "$report"

node "$release/scripts/deadman/send-alert.mjs" --env-file "$ENV_FILE" \
  --subject "[helium/canary] dsh $candidate released — contracts $contracts_status" \
  --body-file "$report"
cat "$report"
````

```bash

  computation engine behind argon's charts. LAYER 3 carries the previous and current
  values of the watched fields.

  degraded subsystem that still serves; `critical` only when the service reports
  itself unhealthy or a dependency is down. Emit the verdict JSON and nothing else.

  Senior (rare, critical only): say in five lines what is down, what downstream
  surface stops working (argon charts, signal queries), and the single next
  diagnostic command. No speculation about causes you cannot see in the payload.
